[P5.3-S] [BUILD] docker_service and docker_stack now have ModifyPlan: pd.gateAPISince (the apiSince annotations above fail the plan on an older daemon) and ownerPlanCheck.
[P5.3-S] [FIXED] bind_options read-back: the daemon reports Propagation `rprivate` for any bind block it stores (docker@v28.5.2/daemon/cluster/convert/container.go:402-406 creates BindOptions for NonRecursive); `rprivate` with an unset `propagation` now reads back null instead of drifting, and a bind mount whose only option is the default `rprivate` no longer shows a `bind_options` block on import.
[P5.3-S] [BUILD] ServiceUpdate `registryAuthFrom`: docker_service / docker_stack updates send `registryAuthFrom=spec` when the provider has no credentials for the image (svcUpdateKeepForce, service_resource.go), as `docker service update` does without --with-registry-auth (docker/cli@v29.9.3 cli/command/service/update.go:216-241); the service actions (actServiceUpdate, action_service.go) send `previous-spec` on a rollback with a previous spec, so the rolled-back tasks pull with the credentials stored with that spec (daemon side: docker@v28.5.2/daemon/cluster/services.go:326-347). Not a user attribute: the provider always sends X-Registry-Auth when it has credentials, which takes precedence.
[P5.3-S] [BUILD] docker_secret: `driver = {name, options}` (SecretSpec.Driver; a driver makes `data`/`data_wo` optional, new config validator secretDriverNeedsData) and `templating = {name, options}` (SecretSpec.Templating, apiSince 1.37, CHANGELOG.md:733); both force replacement (only labels can be updated, SecretUpdate). docker_config: `templating` (ConfigSpec.Templating, CHANGELOG.md:731). Shared helper swarm_driver_s.go.
[P5.3-S] [BUILD] docker_swarm_init: `data_path_addr` (SwarmInit.DataPathAddr, CHANGELOG.md:831; forces replacement; not reported back, kept from config), `ca_config.external_cas [{protocol, url, options, ca_cert}]` (CAConfig.ExternalCAs, init and SwarmUpdate in place; a configured entry without ca_cert matches whatever certificate the daemon reports).
[P5.3-S] [BUILD] docker_swarm_node: `data_path_addr` (SwarmJoin.DataPathAddr, CHANGELOG.md:832; forces replacement except right after import).
[P5.3-S] [BUILD] docker_node: `name` is now Optional+Computed and sets NodeSpec.Name (NodeUpdate); only a configured name is sent (a name computed from the hostname is never written back).
[P5.3-S] [BUILD] Owner label (owner_label.go how-to) wired for docker_secret, docker_config, docker_service, docker_stack (labels on its services, networks, secrets and configs), docker_swarm_init (swarm spec labels) and, per the Lane 0 update, docker_swarm_node (NodeSpec labels set with NodeUpdate after the join): `adopt` attribute, create-time conflict check (swarmOwnerCreateCheck / swarmInitOwnerCreateCheck / swarmNodeOwnerCreateCheck / stackOwnerLabels in swarm_owner_s.go), ownerRecord in Create/Read/Update, ownerPlanCheck in ModifyPlan, ownerStrip on every user-facing labels attribute. A stack records the first foreign owner of any of its objects; with adopt the stack re-submits services whose owner label differs. docker_swarm_init keeps the owner label even when `labels` is unset.

[P5.3-K] [BUILD] Local mode stops ignoring these service keys and maps them onto the Engine calls the provider already makes (new internal/provider/compose_local_k.go, edits in compose_local.go):
[P5.3-K] [BUILD] pull_policy daily, weekly, every_<d>, and refresh with pull_refresh_after now work the way compose v5.1.4 mustPull does (pull.go:350-378): pull when the image is missing or `Metadata.LastTagTime + period` is in the past. The CLI reads the same field (images.go:157). No state is needed: the daemon records the last pull time. A kept container whose image ID changes after such a pull is recreated (convergence.go:337 imageUpdated). Refresh reports "pull_policy <p> is due" as drift, so the next plan shows an update. pull_refresh_after reuses compose-go's every_<d> duration parser (types.go:303-324 GetPullPolicy), so no new module is needed.

This follows compose's bake path (build_bake.go:175-181, :223, :474-483).

TestAccComposeLifecycleK: failed at step 2 on its own assertion. The daemon stores the bare "GONE" in Config.Env as sent (it unsets it only in the process environment), and the check had expected it to be absent. The assertion was fixed.

[P5.3-B] [BUILD] Multi-platform docker_image builds: build.platforms takes several platforms (`docker buildx build --platform a,b`) through the BuildKit seam (frontend attr `platform=a,b`, buildkit_build.go bkFrontendAttrs). imgValidateBuildKit no longer rejects lists; it rejects a comma list inside one element and duplicates (image_build_buildkit.go).
[P5.3-B] [BUILD] Multi-platform result = OCI image index. Loading it needs the containerd image store; checked at apply from GET /info DriverStatus driver-type=io.containerd.snapshotter.v1 (moby daemon/containerd/service.go:109, docker v28.5.2) in buildkit_platforms.go bkMultiPlatformExports. Graph-driver daemons fail before building with a clear error naming the storage driver and the fixes, instead of moby's "exporting multiple references to image store is currently unsupported" (builder/builder-next/exporter/mobyexporter/export.go:109) or BuildKit's "docker exporter does not currently support exporting manifest lists" (buildkit v0.34.2 exporter/oci/export.go:135).
[P5.3-B] [BUILD] On a containerd store: the daemon's BuildKit loads through the moby exporter; other builders' docker-exporter load is switched to the `oci` exporter (tarball into POST /images/load), as buildx does. id = digest = index digest (exporter response containerimage.digest; no containerimage.config.digest for an index), platforms = every platform built; push (outputs type=registry) pushes the index.
[P5.3-B] [BUILD] bake `target.<name>.<attr>` cross-target references (bake-reference.md, buildx_bake_parse.go): target blocks evaluated in reference order (bakeOrderTargets, cycle/self errors), each evaluated target exposed as the `target` variable (own attributes, before inherits; matrix variants by their names); a user variable named `target` is left alone.
[P5.3-B] [BUILD] bake `target:<name>` named contexts (bake-reference.md:438 "Use another target as base"): buildx_bake_contexts.go bkBuildLinked runs one BuildKit gateway build (bkclient.Client.Build): dependencies solved with the dockerfile frontend, each result handed to the next as `context:<name>=input:<name>` with `input-metadata:<name>` carrying containerimage.config (buildkit frontend/dockerui/namedcontext.go:279 case "input"); dependency directories served under their own local names and fed as the frontend's "context"/"dockerfile" inputs (dockerui/context.go:163); multi-platform deps as `<name>::<platform>`. Only the requested target is exported. Secrets/SSH of all steps merged into the one session (conflicting ids are an error). bakeLinkSteps resolves the dependency chain (transitive, cycle error).
[P5.3-B] [BUILD] bake target `resources` applied: buildx keys memory, memory-swap, cpu-shares, cpu-period, cpu-quota, cpuset-cpus, cpuset-mems (buildx_build.md:1173) mapped to the dockerfile frontend options memory, memswap, cpushares, cpuperiod, cpuquota, cpusetcpus, cpusetmems (buildkit v0.34.2 frontend/dockerui/config.go:43-49); sizes take units. `--set <t>.resource.<k>=v` supported. BuildKit applies them only with the exec.meta.linux.resources capability (dockerfile2llb/convert.go:1493); older daemons ignore them silently, as upstream documents.
[P5.3-B] [BUILD] bake target `policy`: still parsed and reported only. PARTIAL kept: Rego evaluation is buildx's policy package on github.com/open-policy-agent/opa (not a dependency); buildkit v0.34.2 only has the transport (client/solve.go:61 SolveOpt.SourcePolicyProvider, sourcepolicy/policysession).
[P5.3-B] [BUILD] bake owner label (Lane 0 update): every target's build labels go through pd.ownerInject (inject-only, no conflict check), action_buildx_bake.go Invoke.
[P5.3-B] [BUILD] docker_buildx_builder driver = "remote" (buildx_create.md:183): endpoint (tcp://, unix://, npipe://) becomes Optional+Computed (required for remote, computed for docker-container), new `remote` block cacert/cert/key/servername (bkclient.WithServerConfig / WithCredentials, buildkit client/client.go:279,290). Create checks ListWorkers when bootstrap, Read reports workers (unreachable: warning, state kept), Delete only forgets. bkOpenNode passes the TLS driver options for remote nodes.
[P5.3-B] [BUILD] build.builder (docker_image) and docker_buildx_bake.builder accept a BuildKit address (anything with "://"): a remote builder's endpoint or docker-container://... (imgSeamBuilder, bake Invoke).
[P5.3-B] [BUILD] docker_buildx_builder kubernetes driver and the buildx instance store: PARTIAL kept (k8s.io/client-go and github.com/docker/buildx/store are not dependencies; neither is BuildKit or Engine API).
[P5.3-B] [BUILD] docker_buildx_history materials: read from the record's provenance attestation in the builder's content store (BuildKit serves it: buildkit control/control.go:194 RegisterContentServer; client: client/client.go:186 ContentClient), SLSA v0.2 predicate.materials and v1 buildDefinition.resolvedDependencies, as `{URI, Digests}` (buildx_history_inspect.md:55). Failure to read is a warning.
[P5.3-B] [BUILD] docker_buildx_history attachment bodies: new `attachment` (digest prefix, `provenance`, `sbom` or a full type), `attachment_platform`, and computed sensitive `attachment_body` (`history inspect attachment`, buildx_history_inspect_attachment.md). The content Read request type is in github.com/containerd/containerd/api (indirect); called by reflection so go.mod stays unchanged (datasource_buildx_history_content.go bxhReadBlob).
[P5.3-B] [BUILD] Owner label: docker_buildx_builder full treatment (adopt attribute, ownerCreateCheck on an existing buildx_buildkit_<node> container, label on the node container and the state volume, ownerRecord in Read, ownerPlanCheck in a new ModifyPlan). docker_image: BuildKit path injects the label (imgBuildViaSeam); classic path and checks need image_resource.go (Lane R): helpers imgOwnerBuildLabels and imgOwnerCreateCheck in image_build_owner.go, see TODO R below. A pulled or imported image cannot be labelled (its config and ID come from the registry or archive): never labelled, never checked.

[P5.3-I] [BUILD] D7: GNUmakefile deleted. Invoke-Build (terraform-provider-docker.build.ps1) is the only entry point, locally and in CI.
[P5.3-I] [BUILD] D7: .github/workflows/ci.yml rewritten: installs InvokeBuild 5.14.23 from the PowerShell Gallery (`Install-Module InvokeBuild -RequiredVersion $env:INVOKE_BUILD_VERSION -Scope CurrentUser -Force`, PSGallery trusted first) under pwsh on ubuntu-latest; every check step is `shell: pwsh` + `Invoke-Build <tasks>`. Action pins (checkout, setup-go, setup-terraform, upload-artifact) unchanged; the bash e2e-gate step unchanged.
[P5.3-I] [BUILD] D7: new build-script tasks for what CI used to run outside make: `ParityCheck` (go run ./tools/parity -check BUILD_REPORT.md), `ApiWatchCheck` (go run ./tools/apiwatch -check). make target -> task: build -> Build, vet -> Vet, test -> Test, check -> Check, e2e -> E2E (Invoke-E2E.ps1), skill -> GenSkill, docs -> Docs, fmt -> Fmt, lint -> Vet + FmtCheck, install -> Install, parity -> Parity, apiwatch -> ApiWatch, compose2stack -> Compose2Stack, harness-up/down -> test/harness/scripts/harness-{up,down}.ps1 (Invoke-E2E.ps1 -KeepUp), testacc -> TestAcc (through the harness; the old make target ran against DOCKER_HOST, against the standing rule).
[P5.3-I] [BUILD] D7: build script resolves `python3` when `python` is not on PATH (GitHub ubuntu images ship python3 only), so Inventory / InventoryValidate / InventoryCheck run in CI with no extra flag. merge.py and validate.py import only the stdlib (argparse, json, os, re, sys, collections).
[P5.3-I] [BUILD] D7: references to make removed from CLAUDE.md (Build and test: CI line rewritten), README.md (Linux/macOS make section replaced by one "Build (Invoke-Build, every platform)" section; make skill / make parity / make compose2stack lines removed; parity list path corrected to tools/parity/paritydata), .claude/skills/engine-api-watch/SKILL.md (`make apiwatch` -> `go run ./tools/apiwatch`; CI step named ApiWatchCheck), build-script header ("The GNUmakefile mirrors these tasks" -> CI order + "There is no Makefile").
[P5.3-I] [ASSUMED] D7: CI's first step is the whole default task (`Invoke-Build`) rather than Build / Vet / Test one by one, so InventoryCheck, InventoryValidate, RegistryCheck, ContractCheck and DocCountsCheck now run in CI (they did not under make), and whatever Lane Z appends to the default task (FieldCoverage in enforce mode) runs in CI with no workflow change. Check then re-runs Vet and GenSkillCheck (about 20 s, accepted for a self-contained Check step). GuideCheck added next to ParityCheck (it is cheap and was not gated anywhere).

[P6a-F] [VERIFIED] Configure already contacted the daemon before this pass: one `GET /_ping` with API negotiation (5s timeout, plus `ssh.connect_timeout` per hop). Any failure was a single generic warning ("Docker daemon not reachable"), and a negotiated version below min_api_version was an error. The ping is kept. Unreachable daemons still give a warning, never an error, so validate, plan and offline use keep working.
[P6a-F] [BUILD] internal/provider/preflight.go: `classifyDaemonError` is the one classification helper. It sorts a failure into not reachable, permission denied, TLS failure or bad response. It checks typed errors first (fs.ErrPermission, EACCES, EPERM, Windows ERROR_ACCESS_DENIED, x509.UnknownAuthorityError, HostnameError, CertificateInvalidError, tls.CertificateVerificationError, RecordHeaderError, AlertError), then the texts produced by the moby client and crypto/tls. The text fallback is needed because moby v0.6.2 drops the error chain on permission denied. `daemonCheckDiagnostic` produces a distinct summary and detail for each class.
[P6a-F] [BUILD] endpointConfig gained HostSource and TLSSource, set by resolveEndpoint. HostSource is the `host` argument, the `ssh` block, Docker context "x" (named by the `context` argument, DOCKER_CONTEXT, or `currentContext` in <dir>/config.json), DOCKER_HOST, or the built-in default. TLSSource is the tls block, `cert_path` <dir>, Docker context, or DOCKER_TLS_VERIFY + DOCKER_CERT_PATH <dir>. The TLS diagnostic lists the settings in effect without secrets: source, CA path (or inline PEM, or system roots), client certificate, skip-verify. It also gives a cause hint: plain HTTP to a TLS daemon, TLS to a plain daemon, unknown CA, hostname mismatch, expired certificate, or a client certificate that is missing or rejected.
[P6a-F] [BUILD] API floor (d): the "Docker daemon too old" error on min_api_version now names the negotiated version, the daemon's reported version, the host and its source, and min_api_version. A daemon below client.MinAPIVersion (1.40) used to fall into the generic "not reachable" warning. It now gets the same error with both versions and the client minimum.
[P6a-F] [BUILD] Unknown values: Configure skips the ping, logging at info level, when a `tls` or `ssh` value or disable_docker_daemon_check is unknown, because the ping would test a different endpoint. host, api_version, context and cert_path were already errors when unknown (unchanged).
[P6a-F] [BUILD] Documented in the provider MarkdownDescription (new "Daemon check" paragraph after the existing text; the first sentence is unchanged) and in the disable_docker_daemon_check description.

## Pass 6b
Goal: proof. Re-pin docker/compose v5.7.0 and docker/cli v29.9.3 (compose parity against the pinned compose binary), example tests for every examples/ folder plus manual scenarios and underscore tasks (Lane E), pairwise combination tests for container / service / network / volume (Lane W), an independent audit of the 93 field skips plus exec stdin and the preflight follow-up (Lane V), then Lane Z with one full PreRelease. Details are in reference/_gen/pass6b/lane-<X>.md and open-findings.md.

[P6b-W] [BUILD] tools/pairwise (Go, stdlib plus modules already in go.mod; go.mod/go.sum unchanged). The schema comes from the provider in-process (providerserver GetProviderSchema, as tools/genskill does): schema.go turns it into a node tree with kind (attribute, nested attribute, block), Terraform type, nesting and max items. A model that names an attribute, a nested key or an object key the provider does not have, a computed-only attribute, or a value of the wrong type fails generation with the known names listed. `-schema <type>` prints the live writable surface for model authors.
[P6b-W] [BUILD] Inspect paths come from the outputs contract, not a second mapping: contract.go reads reference/inventory.json (the source of internal/provider/contract_gen.go). It resolves each model's snake_case `inspect` path through the `fields` of the type's structured `inspect` output (engine:container, engine:network, engine:volume, swarm:service), for example host_config.memory -> HostConfig.Memory. A segment the contract does not list fails while the contract still has fields at that level. Below the listed levels (fields_complete false), it is matched at compare time by normalized name, which is the contract's rule for unlisted levels.
[P6b-W] [BUILD] Model format (reference/_gen/pairwise/<type>.json, documented in tools/pairwise/model.go):
[P6b-W] [BUILD] cover.go: an AETG-style greedy covering array with constraints. Pairs that no valid row can hold are computed by backtracking, counted as excluded and skipped. Each row keeps the best of 30 candidates; a candidate starts from the value with the most uncovered pairs and fills the other factors in shuffled order with the value that covers the most new pairs, never one that leaves the row unsatisfiable. PCG is seeded from the model's seed, so the same model gives byte-identical output.
[P6b-W] [BUILD] gen.go writes <out>/<type>/<nn>/main.tf: a standalone root module with required_providers JerryBalmer1/docker, an empty provider block (DOCKER_HOST from env), variable pw_registry (the harness registry prefix), locals.prefix = pw-<type>-<nn>, and resource "<type>" "pw". It also writes expect.json (assignment and checks, each with its upstream path) and <type>/array.json.
[P6b-W] [BUILD] compare.go:
[P6b-W] [BUILD] tools/pairwise/run.ps1 is the harness-side runner, started by Invoke-E2E.ps1 -Script. It refuses to run unless DOCKER_HOST is tcp://127.0.0.1:12375. Per config: init, apply, show -json, pairwise -compare, plan -detailed-exitcode (when it exits 2: show -json and pairwise -plan-summary), then destroy, which always runs, even after a failed apply. Each step's output goes to <step>.log and the outcome to run.json. Terraform is TF_ACC_TERRAFORM_PATH when the harness sets it (1.14.9 there), else terraform on PATH. Providers come from dev_overrides through TF_CLI_CONFIG_FILE. Images use TF_VAR_pw_registry = "$TF_ACC_REGISTRY_NODES/" (manager:12380/busybox:1.36).

[P6b-V] [ASSUMED] start_dependencies defaults to false, which keeps the previous behaviour (CLI `--no-deps`). The CLI default starts the dependencies, but here they are usually owned by a docker_compose resource, and an action recreating them would fight it. A dependency recreated by run drops its anonymous volumes, as docker_compose's own recreate does; compose's run keeps them (Inherit: true, run.go:309).

[P6b-Z] [FIXED] examples/resources/docker_service never converged: root cause is the healthcheck, not the provider. wget http://localhost/ in nginx:alpine (busybox) goes to ::1, while the mounted docker_config default.conf listens on IPv4 only, so the health check never passes; tasks stay "starting" and swarm stops them with stop_signal SIGQUIT, nginx exits 0 (state complete), and restart_policy on-failure does not restart them. Verified on the harness with variants: full and no-init stay starting; healthcheck CMD true or config not mounted over default.conf both converge 3/3. Fix: healthcheck uses http://127.0.0.1/ (comment explains), and user = "nginx" removed (nginx cannot run as user nginx); the example test no longer replaces user.

[P6c-S] [BUILD] tools/contractgen/swagger.go + swagger_map.json: contractgen derives the whole shape of every mapped structured output from the pinned Engine API swagger (reference/upstream/engine-api/api/swagger.yaml) at generation time: object with properties -> object; object with additionalProperties -> map(T) (keys verbatim); object with only `enum: [{}]` -> object({}) (empty object); array -> list(T); string/enum -> string; integer/number -> number; boolean -> bool; free-form -> dynamic. [P6c-C] [ASSUMED] Builder-group endpoint at the default proxy: buildx v0.38.2 `create` resolves the group into per-platform instances through hub.docker.com/v2/auth/token + /v2/cloud-builds/accounts/<org>/builder-groups/<builder>/instances (create.go:79-210, builder/builder.go:353-367) and gives each instance node its own data-plane proxy and cloud://<org>/<builder>_<platform> endpoint. The provider connects the group itself, <org>/<builder>, at tcp://build-cloud.docker.com:443 with scope builder:<org>/<builder>:build, which is what factory.New does for a node without internal.* driver options (factory.go:89-197). That the service serves a multi-platform build on the group endpoint is assumed (buildx's Features reports MultiPlatform true, driver.go:179-188) and is part of what the live test must show.
[P6c-C] [ASSUMED] gzip compression level: upstream raises the process-global gRPC gzip level to BestCompression (driver.go:42-46); the provider uses the default level so it does not change other gRPC users in the process.

[P6c-F] [BUILD] Item 1 (open finding 3): example tests, manual tasks and PairwiseTest install the provider from a local filesystem mirror instead of dev_overrides. Choice: no goreleaser snapshot (slow); TfDockerTest `Initialize-TfProviderMirror` runs `go build -trimpath -ldflags "-s -w -X main.version=99.0.0"` for the host platform and zips it exactly as GoReleaser does (`terraform-provider-docker_v<ver>[.exe]` inside `terraform-provider-docker_<ver>_<os>_<arch>.zip`) under the packed registry layout `providers/registry.terraform.io/jerrybalmer1/docker/`, plus `terraform-provider-docker_<ver>_SHA256SUMS` and mirror.json. `-ZipPath` publishes a ready zip (e.g. a dist/ zip from Invoke-Build Release) instead. `New-TfMirrorConfig` writes `provider_installation { filesystem_mirror { include = [jerrybalmer1/docker] } direct { exclude = [...] } }`. Built once per run (the first call without TPD_PROVIDER_MIRROR always rebuilds, later calls reuse it through TPD_PROVIDER_MIRROR; ExampleTests points it at test/harness/.logs/examples/mirror-<stamp>, the runner builds it on the first pass). Version 99.0.0 (TPD_MIRROR_VERSION overrides): a release version, since Terraform never selects a prerelease for an unconstrained requirement.
[P6c-F] [BUILD] Invoke-TfExample now runs a real `terraform init` (example copy and prerequisite) and `Assert-TfLockFile` checks .terraform.lock.hcl pins jerrybalmer1/docker at the mirror version with a checksum (zh:<zip sha256> when terraform records zh, else h1). Example copies moved from %TEMP%\tpd-ex-* to test/harness/.work/ex (TPD_EXAMPLE_WORK), kept failure logs too.

[P6c-F] [BUILD] Item 2 (open finding 2): reproduced by construction: replicas had a schema default of 1, so state held replicas = 1 for a global service and `terraform plan -generate-config-out` wrote `replicas = 1`, which ValidateConfig (Pass 6b) rejects for mode global. Fixed: docker_service replicas has no static default; a plan modifier (service_replicas_p6c_f.go svcReplicasDefault) plans 1 for mode replicated, null for the other modes, unknown while mode is unknown, and Read keeps replicas null for every non-replicated mode (svcFlattenOpts.NullReplicasUnlessReplicated). docker_stack service blocks keep the plain default (no such validation there). Existing state of a global service refreshes to null with no planned change.
